- **manako** (manako.was-warning, manako.ostracised, manako.suspected-right, cicada.origins-uncovered): Her cicada.origins-uncovered names 'Manako's tragic history' as the thing the party can uncover; this is the history itself from the same decisions.

- **manako** (manako.its-steering, manako.the-portrait, watcher.what-children-are-for, cicada.farming-it): The Watcher taking his shape and being fed by him is also told in the watcher and cicada files (the children, and 'farming' the Watcher).

- **mayor** (mayor.support-forces-cooperation, kurose-police.jurisdiction-quarrel): That the mayor forces the police and the Directorate to work together on raids is said in both; mayor.support-forces-cooperation (her words) is the better home, and kurose-police.jurisdiction-quarrel (raids-only) adds that sharing a raid isn't sharing a file.

- **mitflit-home-world** (mitflit-home-world.the-problem, mitflits.what-the-wild-find): Two open decisions about Book V that lean on each other: what the home world's own problem is (the-problem), and what the wild mitflits find there and what the High mitflits make of them (what-the-wild-find). Both are due as Book V is prepared, and the seeds item already lists 'the High mitflits don't want the wild ones back', which answers the second.

- **mitflit-home-world** (mitflit-home-world.the-answer, second-rift.starship): That stopping the second rift needs the mitflit home world is said in both; second-rift.starship (her words) is the better home for the need, and the-answer adds that it covers the Watcher too and that only the fuel's makers know how to stop that much.

- **mitflits** (mitflits.wont-explain, era.magic-draining): That magic is slowly draining from Golarion is said in both, from the same v4 note; era.magic-draining is the better home, and wont-explain keeps the taboo and Bobbin's remark that mitflit technology is many times more powerful than terrestrial magic.

- **mitflits** (mitflits.descended-from-crew, generation-ship.crashed): generation-ship.crashed (a colony ship carrying a whole people between the stars) is the better home for who the crew were; descended-from-crew is only the claim that the mitflits are their descendants.

- **mother** (mother.ships-mind, generation-ship.mind-and-eyes, watcher.what-it-is): Mother is the ship's mind, left weakened when the eyes were torn out, is said in three files; watcher.what-it-is is the better home.

- **mother** (mother.met-by-the-four, generation-ship.inside): The reasoning-core chamber on the bridge deck and the cargo deck appear in both; generation-ship is the better home for the ship.

- **mother** (mother.in-every-gen-2, watcher.the-temptation): Two open decisions that ask the same question (whether a piece of Mother is in every Gen 2, as the mechanism for the temptation to use the Watcher's eyes); they could be one.

- **nao** (nao.classmate, beat--naos-file): The chapter 9 beat beat--naos-file already says 'Among Cicada's files is one for Nao, a classmate, who is one of the Faithful': nao.classmate and nao.faithful in one line. Better home for what happens: the beat; for who Nao is: the facts.

- **nao** (nao.turn-in, beat--turn-nao-in): nao.turn-in is an open fact whose decide-by is 'the table decides in play', which is exactly the chapter 9 fork beat--turn-nao-in ('Turn Nao in? Trusted by the Directorate and nobody else, or harbouring a cultist'): not a decision Ingram has to make, so the open fact duplicates the beat.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.kiku, night-regulars.sanzo, night-regulars.tama): canon/plan/chapter-01.md (haunt 11, the Paper Lantern) already says who they are in one line: Kiku the keeper (Dwarf), Sanzo the wandering musician who never quite leaves (Goblin), Tama the young waitress who has never been past the checkpoint (Leshy). The plan is the better home for roles and ancestries; these facts add the look and manner.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.tsubaki, general-hospital.requisitioned): Tsubaki's opening lines (the ward is in a former department store, with the old brass Gentlemen's Outfitting sign over its door) are the building's history, which general-hospital.requisitioned begins (a building requisitioned whole, display windows painted over) and which the hospital scene already tells in full. general-hospital.md is the better home for the building; Tsubaki's fact should keep only her.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.tomoe, directorate-posts.repair-chiefs): Tomoe is the named chief of Refit Crew Three. repair-chiefs says each of the six pilots has a standing repair crew whose chief is unnamed (its six old placeholder NPC files carry the surnames Ishikawa, Kaneda, Kimura, Nakamura, Sato and Watanabe, none of them Hashida), and hulda-brack.md says every repair crew answers to Hulda Brack. Nothing says whether Tomoe is one of the six, a seventh, or the chief of a different crew: a loose end, not a contradiction, and Ingram's call. repair-chiefs is the better home for the crews, this file for her.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.imai, night-regulars.mira, the-telephone-exchange.every-call, directorate-posts.switchboard-supervisor): Imai (thirty-one years on the island's exchange, every call on the island through her board) and Mira (night operator) are people of the exchange that the-telephone-exchange.every-call and directorate-posts.switchboard-supervisor describe (every call connected by hand). The exchange is better homed in the-telephone-exchange.md; the people stay here. A note for that file: its every-call fact has four operators working the wall while its eleven-positions fact has eleven positions, which want squaring.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.aucourt, night-regulars.chiyo, night-regulars.daisuke, night-regulars.grenna, night-regulars.imai, night-regulars.kiku, night-regulars.morishita, night-regulars.petr, night-regulars.sanzo, night-regulars.sayo): Ten facts end with a register sentence copied word for word from voice.register on the same person's ink cast card in the haunt scene, and most of the 37 facts that have a card also repeat its tics in near-identical words, although D075 said the facts would hold only what the cards do not. The cast card is the better home for voice; these facts need only look and ancestry.

- **night-regulars** (night-regulars.technical-track-student): Not a restatement: a loose end between pencil and ink. The Canteen's 2026-10-01 re-read cut the technical-track candidate (canon/scenes/episode-c1e1--canteen.reread.md), so this fact describes someone no scene casts, and it is the only fact in the file with no cast card. Ingram's call: keep the person for a later scene, or cut.

- **nirvana** (nirvana.luminara, nirvana.the-relic, founders.luminara): founders.luminara tells the same reception (Sarenrae in Luminara's cathedral, 'through an act not born of intent but of consequence', sent to mend the tear and be returned); founders.luminara is the better home for the reception, nirvana for the place.

- **nirvana** (nirvana.djarvik, founders.the-others): founders.the-others also has Djarvik, a gnoll sorcerer who joined before Nirvana.

- **nobu** (nobu.lab-assistant, manako.revenge-list): manako.revenge-list says Nobu saw it forty-four years ago and said nothing; the same event from Manako's side. Better home: nobu.lab-assistant for what Nobu did, manako.revenge-list for the list.

- **nobu** (nobu.funds-the-column, the-fixer.books): the-fixer.books describes the same trail as Cicada's money running through the crime lords and back into the Directorate's procurement, to Nobu; read literally that is the opposite direction to Nobu funding the column out of procurement (a loop would reconcile them). baldwin.manako-works-for-him and baldwin.manakos-boss both repeat 'Nobu's laundering'. Better home: nobu for the funding, the-fixer.books for the evidence.

- **nobu** (nobu.paying-a-debt, caretaker.misguided-normalcy): caretaker.misguided-normalcy and caretaker.name-ume say every kindness she wins is charged to the technician-day pool; nobu.paying-a-debt says Nobu holds that pool and she has to beg him for it. Same pool from two sides; Nobu's file is the better home for who holds it.

- **nobu** (nobu.knows-whose, baldwin.nobu-knows, the-fixer.how-much-he-sees): Three open decisions ask who in the chain can see whose organisation the money is washed through, with different candidate lists (nobu.knows-whose: he knows it's Manako's | he doesn't; baldwin.nobu-knows: nobody knows | the Fixer sees more than he says | Nobu knows; the-fixer.how-much-he-sees: he sees the names | only the routes | has never looked), two of them due before Chapter 9 is written. Canon says the crime lords' organisation is Baldwin's (baldwin.manako-works-for-him), so 'Manako's' may be older wording. One question should own it.

- **nobu** (nobu.looks-away, episode-c1e1--kit-issue): The Episode One Kit Issue scene already gives Nobu the voice 'harried and fast, in run-on sentences; does the generous thing too quickly to be thanked for it', pushes a handlamp at everyone, and runs three conversations at once. looks-away and quartermaster say the same in more words. Better home for the play: the scene.

- **nobu** (nobu.opinions-on-gear, episode-c1e1--kit-issue): The Kit Issue scene already has Nobu's favourites as a topic and an extra earned only by asking him properly (a specific request, fitted to the player); opinions-on-gear is the same advice written as a character note. Better home: the scene.

- **nobu** (nobu.quartermaster, nobu.off-book): The ink fact off-book already explains the small unexplained generosity (extras bought for cash from the waterfront chandlers); quartermaster only names the trait.

- **noodle-stand-owner** (noodle-stand-owner.the-late-stand, rest--the-paper-lantern): The Paper Lantern already does what the stand is for (a civilian counter off Directorate ground where staff and trainees drink side by side, a keeper who weighs you for a second and serves everyone alike); the noodle stand owner looks like an earlier version of Kiku and the Lantern. Ingram may want the stand folded into the Lantern or kept as a second place.

- **nozomi** (nozomi.first-pilot, nozomi.the-show, directorate.why-brought-forward, directorate.missing-pilot-search): Two decided facts already say it: directorate.why-brought-forward ('Nozomi, the pilot expected to fly first, refused the draft after a magic show') and directorate.missing-pilot-search; these pencil pieces restate that ink.

- **nozomi** (nozomi.detained, nozomi.her-account, beat--nozomi-in-detention, beat--find-nozomi-early): The Chapter 2 plan beats beat--nozomi-in-detention and beat--find-nozomi-early say the same things (she is in detention; she gives them Cicada's argument from someone they trust); the beats are the better home for what happens, the facts for what is true.

- **nozomi** (nozomi.shown-the-watcher, the-young-one.went-to-a-show, cicada.the-show): The show itself (the Watcher revealed, proof it is real, an honest choice) is also told in cicada.the-show and as the Young One's mirror in the-young-one.went-to-a-show; cicada.the-show is the better home for how a show works.

- **nozomi** (nozomi.locker, directorate.paperwork-disappears-people): directorate.paperwork-disappears-people already says 'Nozomi, who declined the draft, is held in questioning while her name stays on the roster'.

- **nozomi** (nozomi.peril, hori.peril): Peril Roster entries (v4:21) are filed one per person across files; the roster could have a single home instead.

- **party** (party.worst-jobs, section-officer.the-drains): Section Nine gets the worst jobs on the base is already what the ink fact section-officer.the-drains shows (the night's worst job, given in front of the hall) and what section-officer.hazing says Vetch does to every new section; plan/books.md also opens Book I with 'the worst jobs, Gen 1 clankers, and a petty superior'. Better home: the section-nine thread or the Book I line.

- **prophecy** (prophecy.conditions-not-right, second-rift.what-sets-it-off): conditions-not-right (hers) already says the players and the antagonists bring together the right mix of things for the rift to happen, which narrows the open decision second-rift.what-sets-it-off (the Watcher uses it when cornered, or it goes off by accident).

- **rift** (rift.the-fumble, exotic-matter.what-it-is, shucks.kortos-survey, founders.the-plea): The four dropping the fuel container on the generation ship's cargo deck is also told in these files, and the decided fact rift.cause already says it; the throw-and-catch details exist only here.

- **rift** (rift.flung-to-nirvana, founders.a-week, nirvana.luminara): The four's trip to Nirvana is told again in the founders and nirvana files; those are the better home for what happened there.

- **rika** (rika.refused-the-explanation, the-sceptics.who): the-sceptics.who says the sceptics are the minority at Manako's conference who did not accept the mass-hallucination explanation, and investigated privately for decades: Rika is the named one of them. Better home: the-sceptics.who for the group, Rika's file for her own notes.

- **rika** (rika.insufficient-persistence, nobu.lab-assistant): insufficient-persistence sets Rika's atoning by investigating against Nobu's by funding; the Nobu half is also in nobu.the-debt and nobu.lab-assistant. The pairing lives only in Rika's file.

- **rupiah** (rupiah.asked-the-gods, prophecy.asked): prophecy.asked (decided, ink: after the rift, Rupiah asked the gods whether it would ever happen again) already says it; the pencil restates it in her words and is redundant once that is inked.

- **rupiah** (rupiah.public-face, rupiah.cuts-the-ribbon, rupiah.role): public-face (Claude) restates two ink facts, that Rupiah cuts the Founding Day ribbon and works in the Directorate's administration, and adds Senior Advisor, charming, the civil defence anthem and that the party only comes to know him late.

- **rupiah** (rupiah.diplomacy-with-mitflits, founders.diplomacy-first): Both say diplomacy rather than violence was the founders' way with the mitflits; rupiah.diplomacy-with-mitflits (her words) is the better home for Rupiah's part, founders.diplomacy-first for the old campaign's Otari sessions.

- **sap** (sap.released-where-children-form, watcher.forms-where-sap): The same Book I rule (children can form only where Sap has been released); watcher.forms-where-sap is the better home, with Cicada's demonstrations releasing Sap kept here.

- **sap** (sap.what-it-is, generation-ship.sap-store, sap.catalogued-at-kortos): That Sap is the generation ship's stock of raw material for its repair nanites is said three times; sap.what-it-is is the better home.

- **sap** (sap.catalogued-at-kortos, manako.inert-12): Both say Manako was handed the shelved Inert 12 sample (about 45 and about 44 years ago); manako.inert-12 is the better home for his part.

- **sap** (sap.makes-it-visible, lens.treated-with-sap): The same claim with the same metadata (known by few, told later); sap.makes-it-visible is the better home.

Same number, same fact, from the teacher's side.

- **school-civil-defence-instructor** (school-civil-defence-instructor.drills, directorate-posts.school-civil-defence-instructor): directorate-posts.school-civil-defence-instructor is the same paragraph, almost word for word, as drills and refused-four-times together (eleven years, four alert states, nothing used in a hundred and fifty years, applied four times and refused on cost), all marked known by some where this file separates the some from the few. Better home: this file; the directorate-posts entry can go.

- **school-civil-defence-instructor** (school-civil-defence-instructor.refused-four-times, directorate-posts.school-civil-defence-instructor): The same duplicate, for the second half: applying four times to have the alert-state drill reinstated is in both, with a different known (few here, some there).

- **school-history-teacher** (school-history-teacher.says-i-dont-know, episode-c1e1--school-first-period): The Episode One first-period scene already gives Mister Ota the voice 'says I don't know plainly and doesn't mind'. Better home: the scene.

- **school-natural-history-teacher** (school-natural-history-teacher.collector, episode-c1e1--school-second-period): The Episode One second-period scene already has her room smelling of seawater and preserving jars, thirty years of the city's drains and tide-line, and a voice that is fast, delighted and loves I don't know. Better home for the play: the scene.

- **search-ship** (search-ship.still-calling, watcher.drawn-by-the-call, watcher.drifted-to-kurose): That the buried ship's call drew the Watcher across the world is said in three places; search-ship.still-calling is the better home for the call, watcher.drifted-to-kurose for the journey.

- **search-ship** (search-ship.second-excavation, directorate.first-gen-1): Both have the Directorate digging up the search ship to recover its technology and lock it away.

- **second-rift** (second-rift.learns-the-worth, second-rift.takes-the-matter, second-rift.the-violet-knot, second-rift.opens): These four are the steps of the 'Second Impact' clock in threads/the-watcher.md, whose 'By Book' IV line restates them; the clock is the better home for their order.

- **second-rift** (second-rift.gap-is-after, second-rift.the-gap, second-rift.gap-name): Three items about the Gap (one pencil, two open): gap-is-after (hers) already says it happens after the adventure, perhaps as a result of it, which narrows the open question of whether the Gap is how the story ends.

- **second-rift** (second-rift.what-sets-it-off, prophecy.conditions-not-right): conditions-not-right (hers) already says the players and the antagonists bring together the right mix of things for the rift; second-rift.what-sets-it-off (open) asks the same thing again.

- **section-officer** (section-officer.never-wrong, section-officer.vetch): The ink fact vetch already says he is never wrong in his own account; never-wrong repeats it and adds the one-beat pause, the recasting as intention and the way a player can pin him. Better home: vetch for the claim, never-wrong for the play detail.

- **section-officer** (section-officer.voice, section-officer.never-wrong, episode-c1e1--assembly, episode-c1e1--canteen): The Episode One scenes already play these two habits: in Assembly he collects them as 'Section Nine. With me.' and tells them to read the sheet, and in the canteen a slow handclap makes him pause one beat and then thank the hall as if it were his plan. The scenes are the home for the play; the facts keep the rule.

- **section-officer** (section-officer.the-transmission-chain, section-officer.hazing): The ink fact hazing already says the fool's errand was done to Vetch and to Manako decades ago; the transmission chain repeats it and adds the lab, the shrug and 'nobody says it aloud'. It dates the lab 'forty years ago' where Manako's own facts say about forty-four. Better home: hazing for the claim.

- **section-officer** (section-officer.the-transmission-chain, the-young-one.competitive): the-young-one.competitive repeats the same thesis in a line (Manako panicked in a lab, the Section Officer was hazed and passes it down, this boy builds a belief on it): the transmission chain is the fuller statement and the Young One is the third generation.

- **section-officer** (section-officer.has-it-in-for-them, section-officer.drains-for-the-vial): The ink fact drains-for-the-vial already makes the drains Vetch's answer to the vial requisition (or the laugh); has-it-in-for-them is the same grudge carried forward as ever-worse duties.

- **shinobu** (shinobu.released, kudo.the-lawyer): kudo.the-lawyer says he fought her out of military detention once, very good at his job and very loud about it: the same sentence as shinobu.released, told from his side.

- **shinobu** (shinobu.observed, the-watcher): The Observed thread does not exist in canon; the-watcher thread already lists shinobu among its entities, so shinobu.observed repeats what the thread file says.

- **shinobu** (shinobu.detained, directorate.paperwork-disappears-people): directorate.paperwork-disappears-people already names Shinobu Kudo as detained without charge, a department doing an ordinary thing. Better home for her case: shinobu.detained.

- **shucks** (shucks.on-the-list, shucks.supervised-the-disgrace, manako.disgraced-after-the-spill, manako.revenge-list, manako.list-worked-by-inner-circle): That Shucks supervised Manako's disgrace forty-four years ago and is on his revenge list is told five times across two files: twice here and three times in manako.md (disgraced-after-the-spill; revenge-list, which names Shucks and Nobu; list-worked-by-inner-circle, which calls Shucks the institutional author of his ruin). Better home: manako.md, the person who keeps the list; shucks.md need only keep the open question of what Shucks makes of it (knows-hes-a-target).

- **shucks** (shucks.forbidden-to-explain, directorate.who-decided-the-silence): The open decision in directorate.md asks who decided that candidates mustn't be told what the blood screen tests for; forbidden-to-explain (pencil, merged here into screen-and-silence) already says the silence is the institution's, not Shucks's, because the Directorate doesn't want anyone to know who its compatible pilots are before it does. Better home: the open decision; the pencil fact is leaning on it.

- **shucks** (shucks.kortos-survey, rift.cause): The four dropping a container of exotic fuel on the cargo deck of the generation ship at Kortos is rift.cause (decided, D029/D032/D040); kortos-survey restates it as the content of an old paper (and adds that the ship's navigation and sensor system has been missing ever since). It is also the core road of the question how-first-impact-happened, so it must stay its own item. Better home for the accident: rift.cause; kortos-survey keeps only the survey and the missing system.

- **shucks** (shucks.at-first-impact, rift.cause, rift.entered-absalom): Shucks at First Impact fetching exotic fuel, and at Pharasma's temple when the four stopped the rupture, overlaps rift.md (the rift cut through a temple to Pharasma; the four caused it with a dropped container). rift.md is the better home for the event, shucks.md for what Shucks was doing.

- **shucks** (shucks.rand-the-hand, shucks.rand, shucks.old-handwriting): Pencil restating ink: the first clause of rand-the-hand (merged here into who-rand-is), 'Rand, Shucks's detached right hand', is the ink fact shucks.rand, and 'writes in Shucks's old handwriting' is the ink fact shucks.old-handwriting. What the pencil adds is the kite, the paper archive and that the Watcher has never read it.

- **shucks** (shucks.rand-the-override, shucks.rand-signs, shucks.rand-signature): Pencil partly restating ink: 'countersigned the party's requisition' in rand-the-override is the ink fact shucks.rand-signs (Rand countersigns the vial requisition the night they file it) and shucks.rand-signature (the green-ink countersignature Vetch is holding). What the pencil adds is the roster, the Medical order putting the six on familiarisation, and the chalk on the board.

- **shucks** (shucks.rand-ways-to-find-out, shucks.old-handwriting, shucks.rand-signature): The four ways to learn who Rand is include two that the ink facts already set up (the handwriting beside anything the founders signed; the countersignature), and the question who-is-rand lists them as roads; the pencil fact is the fuller list of four. No change needed beyond keeping them aligned.

- **shucks** (shucks.standing-with-the-deputy, shucks.not-a-guardian-angel, shucks.tells-the-deputy): The open decision standing-with-the-deputy says 'He must not become their protector whatever the answer', which not-a-guardian-angel already holds; and idea--a-doctor-without-an-appointment (ideas/directorate-episode-two.md) is the scene built on tells-the-deputy. The open decision could carry just the question.

- **stray** (stray.what-it-is, stray.visible, idea--the-stray-as-tonal-keystone): idea--the-stray-as-tonal-keystone (ideas/sweep-v4.md) says the stray is a companion that follows one character around waiting to be fed, a small broken-off piece of the Watcher; it restates stray.what-it-is and is the older source of the open stray.visible.

- **stray** (stray.where-it-comes-from, stray.raised): Both also appear as plan beats in nearly the same words (beat--the-stray and beat--feed-the-stray, Chapter 5): the plan holds the moment, the fact holds the world state.

- **taka** (taka.claims-the-table, episode-c1e1--canteen): The Episode One canteen scene already has Taka arrive with two chairs, claim the six as his table and ask each where they are properly from, and its notes say his warmth is inclusion, never nicknames. Better home: the scene.

- **taka** (taka.warm-because-free, nobu.looks-away): taka.warm-because-free names Shucks and Nobu as the warm adults worn down by the institution, nobu.looks-away contrasts Nobu's guilty generosity with Shucks's infinite-time warmth, and directorate.decent-adults lists Shucks, Nobu and Taka together as warm and on the children's side. One triangle of three kindnesses written in three places; Ingram may want it in one.

- **the-drain-head** (the-drain-head.the-cover, directorate-posts.landing-end-duty-nco): directorate-posts.landing-end-duty-nco covers the 'drain-cover checks after a high tide' on a quiet night shift: the post that is supposed to look at the Drain Head, in the same breath as the visits that are never scheduled.

- **the-fixer** (the-fixer.books, nobu.whose-money, nobu.funds-the-column, nobu.the-supply-line): The Directorate's procurement money washed through the crime lords to pay for the column is the Fixer's books from Nobu's side; nobu.whose-money is the better home for the money flow, the-fixer.books for the clue where the party sees it.

- **the-fixer** (the-fixer.how-much-he-sees, baldwin.nobu-knows, nobu.knows-whose): Three open decisions ask nearly the same question, who in the chain can see whose organisation the money runs through (the Fixer; Nobu or the Fixer; Nobu), two of them with the same decide-by (before Chapter 9 is written); they should be answered as one.

- **the-fixer** (the-fixer.logistics, the-fixer.audit): Both also appear as plan beats in nearly the same words (beat--the-warehouse-heist in Chapter 4: hook, chain, crowbar and 'It's already been signed for'; beat--the-audit in Chapter 9).

- **the-plot-room** (the-plot-room.always-behind, directorate-posts.plot-room-supervisor): directorate-posts.plot-room-supervisor says the supervisor is responsible for how out of date the city's picture is and acutely aware of it: the same lag, from the person's side. The place file is the better home for the lag.

- **the-registry** (the-registry.typed-per-clearance, the-registry.the-typists, directorate-posts.registry-clerk): directorate-posts.registry-clerk says the same twice over: every document is typed once per clearance level, and the clerk has read every version and holds no clearance worth the name. The place file is the better home; the post keeps the role.

- **the-rock** (the-rock.warden-and-director, directorate-posts.the-rock-warden, directorate-posts.the-rock-research-director): The warden and the research director are in three places: this one-line fact (known by few) and the two longer post entries in directorate-posts.md (known by some). Better home for the people: directorate-posts.md (one post per person); the-rock.md need only say that The Rock has a warden and a research director. I have folded the one-liner into the merged item here because it says the same as the opening sentence of each post.

- **the-rock** (the-rock.when-it-was-cut, central-fortification.what-is-in-the-rock, central-fortification.who-built-it): Three open decisions in two files all ask about the same rock and when it was cut: central-fortification.what-is-in-the-rock (does anything lie in the rock Central is cut from), central-fortification.who-built-it (who built Central and when) and the-rock.when-it-was-cut (before Central, alongside it, or later). They could be answered in one go.

- **the-sceptics** (the-sceptics.who, rika.refused-the-explanation, rika.believed-by-nobody): Doctor Rika is the one named sceptic: rika.refused-the-explanation and believed-by-nobody are the group's who, for her, and are also the plan beat beat--the-sceptics-dinner; keep both, but the group fact should name her as its first member.

- **the-sceptics** (the-sceptics.conclusion, manako.wrong-model): manako.wrong-model says the truth lives in Hori, the museum fragment, the sceptics and the Watcher's own behaviour; the sceptics' conclusion (semi-sentient, benign and malleable) is that truth from their side; adjacent, not a duplicate.

- **the-soldier** (the-soldier.former-instructor, the-soldier.on-the-cordon, aoi.unidentified): aoi.unidentified ('filed as one deceased, unidentified') is the same filing the Soldier helped make; aoi.unidentified is the better home for the filing itself, known by few in Chapter 11, the Soldier's file for his part in it.

- **the-soldier** (the-soldier.how-he-knows, the-young-one.cicadas-source): Both say the Young One is Cicada's source inside the Directorate (and so how the Soldier's people know 'Section Nine'); the-young-one.cicadas-source is the better home for the source, the Soldier's fact for the consequence.

- **the-soldier** (the-soldier.never-knew-the-name, hori.cleared-in-court): Both tie Aoi's name to the court proceeding (Hori learned it there; the Soldier had no part in it); adjacent, not duplicates.

- **the-soldier** (the-soldier.the-squad, the-soldier.his-side, the-soldier.wants-to-be-caught): Each is also a plan beat in nearly the same words (beat--meet-the-cadre in Chapter 3; beat--the-soldiers-arrest and beat--what-to-offer-the-soldier in Chapter 11): the plan holds the moment, the fact holds the world state.

- **the-telephone-exchange** (the-telephone-exchange.every-call, directorate-posts.switchboard-supervisor): directorate-posts.switchboard-supervisor repeats that every call between any two parts of the Directorate is physically connected by an operator, making them the best-informed people on the island almost by accident. The place file is the better home.

- **the-works** (the-works.the-canteen, directorate-posts.canteen-manager): Both say the canteen feeds the whole works strip in three sittings and treats rank as a suggestion; the manager 'knows roughly everything about how the work is really going' as the canteen says the fitters do. The place file is the better home; the post keeps the person.

- **the-works** (the-works.the-canteen, episode-c1e1--canteen): The ink scene Canteen is set in 'the Long Hall, the base's canteen', with several hundred candidates at long tables; the-works.the-canteen is 'seventy metres of tables in three sittings' where everyone who makes a Frame eats. They may be one building under two names or two canteens; nothing says which.

- **the-works** (the-works.erecting-shop, directorate-posts.works-manager): directorate-posts.works-manager decides what gets built and in which bay, which is the crane-schedule and bay-naming power described in erecting-shop; the manager's side is the post's, the room's side the place file's.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.went-to-a-show, nozomi.the-show, nozomi.shown-the-watcher, nozomi.stayed): The Young One is written as the Nozomi mirror, and each of the two files tells the show from its own side (the same revelation, an honest choice); nozomi.* is the better home for her show, the Young One's file for his.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.the-killing, the-young-one.doctrine-afterwards, manako.stops-asking): manako.stops-asking restates the killing from Manako's side (he finds out afterwards and does nothing); adjacent rather than duplicate.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.doctrine-afterwards, the-young-one.competitive, section-officer.the-transmission-chain, idea--nobody-did-evil): The 'nobody has done anything evil, they flinch and justify the flinch' thesis (Manako, the Section Officer, the Young One) is stated in four places; idea--nobody-did-evil (ideas/watcher-the-swarm.md) is the better home for the theme and section-officer.the-transmission-chain for the hazing.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.cicadas-source, the-soldier.how-he-knows): Both say the Young One is Cicada's source inside the Directorate (the Soldier's file for how his people know 'Section Nine'); the-young-one.cicadas-source is the better home for the source.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.classmate, the-young-one.canteen, the-young-one.looks): The Episode One scene episode-c1e1--canteen (awaiting her read) already plays this person as 'a pilot-track candidate at the next table' whom its notes name the Young One, an ordinary, slightly smug classmate; these pencil facts restate the scene and add what it does not say (his Frame-pilot standing, that he is an elf). Play material may not cite pencil, so the scene's version is the one that counts until she inks these.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.classmate, the-young-one.name): classmate ends 'His name isn't chosen yet', which restates the open fact the-young-one.name; the open fact is the home.

- **the-young-one** (the-young-one.the-killing, the-young-one.unmasked): Both also appear as plan beats in the same words (beat--the-killing in Chapter 10; beat--the-young-one-unmasked in Chapter 12).

- **transparency-advocate** (transparency-advocate.refused-three-times, directorate.vault-inventory-refused): The same fact in two files: the Advocate has asked three times for the deep-storage inventory and been told it is above their clearance. They differ in known (few here, some in directorate.md). Better home: transparency-advocate.md (it is the person's own fact), with directorate.vault-goal pointing at it.

- **transparency-advocate** (transparency-advocate.satoru, directorate.the-three-poles): The open decision directorate.the-three-poles re-describes the Advocate (and the Commander and the Caretaker) in its own text, and asks whether to keep the Advocate as written. The person file already holds the description, so the open decision could carry only the question.

- **vermilion-block** (vermilion-block.who-knows, vermilion-block.the-silos, directorate-posts.vermilion-block-custodian, directorate-posts.vermilion-block-director): The custodian who signs for the silos and knows exactly how much and nothing about what it is for, and the director whose sign-off is the one that means anything, are told again as posts in directorate-posts. The place file is the better home for the silos and who knows; the posts keep the roles.

- **vermilion-block** (vermilion-block.how-it-looks, directorate-posts.vermilion-block-director): how-it-looks has a red diamond on the roof; the director post describes the black-and-red building 'marked with a red diamond hazard placard'. Likely the same diamond, said two ways.

- **watcher** (watcher.how-it-looks, lens.what-it-shows): Both say through a lens the Watcher is a sky of eyes on stalks and slow tentacles, with the same known (few) and told (chapter 6); lens.what-it-shows is the better home, since it also carries the lens.

- **watcher** (watcher.children, sap.children-made-of-it, lens.never-without): All three say the children are built from Sap and solid enough for anyone to see; watcher.children is the better home, and I merged sap.children-made-of-it into sap.what-it-is because it is told in chapter 9.

- **watcher** (watcher.forms-where-sap, sap.released-where-children-form): The same Book I rule (children form only where Sap has been released), plus Cicada's demonstrations releasing it; watcher.forms-where-sap is the better home.

- **watcher** (watcher.mass-clock, sap.grows-the-watcher, sap.gauge): The things that move the Mass Clock (every use of Sap, demonstrations, the column) are sap.grows-the-watcher's list, and 'show it once they understand Sap' is sap.gauge; watcher.mass-clock is the better home.

- **watcher** (watcher.what-it-is, generation-ship.mind-and-eyes, mother.ships-mind): All say the Watcher is the ship's eyes and Mother its mind, torn apart at First Impact; watcher.what-it-is is the better home (it is the Watcher's own definition).

- **watcher** (watcher.native-to-the-phones, generation-ship.technology-leaked, era.dug-up-not-invented): That the phones and the Directorate's electronics run on ship technology that leaked from Kortos is said in three files; era.dug-up-not-invented is the better home, since it also holds the restriction and the 1950s level.

- **watcher** (watcher.drawn-by-the-call, watcher.drifted-to-kurose, search-ship.still-calling): That the buried ship's call drew the Watcher across the world is said in three places; search-ship.still-calling is the better home for the call, watcher.drifted-to-kurose for the journey.

- **watcher** (watcher.hidden-numbers, lens.superstitions, lens.always-works): That the lens modifier is never shown, so players build wrong theories, is in both lens.superstitions and hidden-numbers; the lens file is the better home for it.

- **watcher** (watcher.sightings, sap.makes-it-visible, lens.treated-with-sap): That the chemical which makes the Watcher briefly visible is Sap is said in three files; sap.makes-it-visible is the better home.

- **watcher** (watcher.the-temptation, mother.in-every-gen-2): Two open decisions that ask the same question: whether a piece of Mother is in every Gen 2, as the mechanism for the temptation to use the Watcher's eyes; they could be one open decision.

- **watcher** (watcher.why-plotted-early, watcher.plotted-before-the-alert): The open decision restates the pencil fact in its first sentences (contacts plotted before the alert that reports them); the pencil fact is the observation, the open one asks what it means.
